Implications found by an exploration (equations_order3.json, 644 questions, Vampire expert, 10 s to prove, 1 s and 10 s for a model, 1 s per other law of a free magma, Mace4 30 s for models, ETP graph as background and its refutations as objects, duals of unconfirmed implications asked).
background = derived by equational reasoning or taken from the ETP graph; confirmed =
no counterexample in the searched sizes, or proved by Vampire; unconfirmed = neither
refuted nor decided;
mapped = dual of another implication, added when not already entailed; mapped unconfirmed
= dual of an unconfirmed implication. Rejected implications are shown false as a whole
without knowing which law of their conclusion fails; tentatively rejected ones are
undecided and taken to be false. Joint conflicts are minimal sets of unconfirmed
implications that contradict an object or a rejected implication only together.
